STATE BANK OF INDIA 
Central Office 

NOTICES 

Bombay , the 16th September 1970 

In pursuance of Regulation 76(1) of the State Bank of 
India General Regulations, 1955, the Executive Com- 
mittee of the Central Board has empowered the Officer-in- 
Charge, O & M Section, Hyderabad Local Head Office, 
to exercise the signing powers specified therein. 

By order of the Executive Committee 
of the Central Board 

The following appointment on the Bank’s Staff is hereby 
notified : — 

Shri C. V. Viswanathan has been appointed as De- 
puty Branch Inspector on the Central Office staff as 
from the 16th September 1970. 

T. R. VARADACHARY 

Managing Director 


STATE BANK OF TRAVANCORE 

(Subsidiary of the State Bank of India) 
Incorporated in India under Special Statute 
The Liability of the Members is limited 
Head Office : Trivandrum 
NOTICE 

Trivandrum the 12 th August 1970 
With reference to the Notice of the 22nd June 1970 
issued in terms of Regulation 30(2) of the Subsidiary 
Banks General Regulations, regarding the holding o'f a 
General Meeting of the Shareholders of the State Bank 
of Travancore at the Head Office of the Bank, for the 
purpose of electing a person to be a Director on the 
Board of the Bank in pursuance of Section 33(1) (b) of 
the State Bank of India (Subsidiary Banks) Act, 1959, 
read with clause (d) of sub-section (1) of Section 25 
of the Act (ibid), to fill the vacancy caused by the resig- 
nation of Shri S. V. Pandit, NOTICE IS HEREBY 
GIVEN that I have accepted as valid the nomination pro- 
posing the name of Shri Kayalath Chacko Kuruvilla as a 
candidate for election as Director on the Board of die 
State Bank of Travancore. The said nomination t>eing 
the only valid nomination received, Shri KayalatA Chacko 


Part III— Sec, 4j 


THE GAZETTEpF INDIA, OCTOBER 3, 1970 (ASVINA 11, 1892) 


1097 


KifSivilla, “Kayalath”, Mount Pleasant, Engineering 
College P.O., Trivandrum- 16, shall be deemed to be elect- 
ed Director on the Board of the Bank at the said General 
Meeting of the Shareholders proposed to be held on Wed- 
nesday the Twenty-sixth day of August 1970, which Meet- 
ing in terms of Regulation 33(0 nf said General 
Regulations now stands cancelled. 

D. VARMA 
leral Manager 

THE INSTITUTE OF CHARTERED ACCOUNTANTS OF 
INDIA 

(Chartered Accountants) 

New Delhi-1, the 1 8//i September 1970 

No. l-CA(37)/l/70 — In exercise of the powers confer- 
red by clause (ii) of Part II of the Second Schedule to 
the Chartered Accountants Act, 1949 (38 of 1949), the 
Council of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of 
India hereby makes the following amendment in the 
notification of the Institute of Chartered Accountants of 
India No. l-CA(37)/70, dated the 23rd May, 1970, 
namely : — 

In the said notification, the words brackets and 
figures “and pursuant to directions issued by Gov- 
ernment (vide their letter No. 52/3/67-CL.II, dated 
the 5th May, 1970)”, shall be omitted. 

C. BALAKRISHNAN 
Secretary 

THE INSTITUTE OF COST AM) WORKS 
ACCOUNTANTS OF INDIA 

(Cost Accountants) 

Calcutta-16, the 14 th September 1970 

No. 19-CWA (33)/ 70 — In exercise of the powers con- 
ferred by sub-section (1) of Section 30 of the Cost and 
Works Accountants Act, 1959, (Act No. 23 6f 1959), 
the Council of the Institute of Cost and Works Accoun- 
tants of India has made the following amendments in the 
Cost and Works Accountants Regulations, 1959, the same 
having been previously published and approved by the 
Central Government as required by sub-section (3) of the 
said Section. 

In the said Regulations, for the existing sub-regulation 
(2) of Regulation 21 including its two provisos, the 
following shall be substituted and shall be deemed to have 
been so substituted with effect from 1st April 1970 : — 

“(2) Every registered student shall pay an annual 
subscription of Rs. 20/- which shall be due and pay- 
able on the 1st April in each year : 

Provided that half the amount of the annual subs- 
cription shall be payable for the first year by a stu- 
dent admitted on or after the 1st October and before 
the 1st April next following.” 

S. N. GHOSE 
Secretary
